📖 LEARNED: Reading Weather (Level 2). Falling pressure = weather coming. Your joints ache? That's a barometer. So are the birds — silent birds mean storm.
📖 LEARNED: Animal Behavior (Level 2). Predators test: they approach, watch, decide. Prey freezes or bolts. Learn which you're looking at.
📖 LEARNED: Wire Snares (Level 2). Set the loop at head height for the target species. Anchor it solid. Camouflage the wire.
📖 LEARNED: Food Preservation (Level 2). Cold smoking (under 90°F) preserves without cooking. Jerky done right lasts months.
📖 LEARNED: Cordage (Level 2). Reverse-wrap technique makes rope strong enough to hold weight. Braid for more strength.
📖 LEARNED: Reading the Land (Level 2). Edges are the grocery store — where meadow meets woods, where water meets bank. Disturbed ground regrows first and fattest.
